### Concrete Architectural, Performance & Incremental-Reuse Bottlenecks Identified So Far

#### Issue A (`gofresh/internal/closure`): `inProgScope` and `inCycleScope` Completely Disabled Whenever `reach.enumeratedRoot` Is True (`freshparam.go:494-495`, `dynamic_walk.go:249-250`, `dispatch_targets.go:120`)
- **Evidence from `/tmp/pew-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  - During `PrepareTestUnit` (`18:17:23 .. 18:23:27`), `ComputeObservabilityBatch` took **`6m04s`** across `273` test subjects in `pew/cmd/pew` (~`1.0s` per subject), emitting **`685,000+` `closure.invoke.unclosed`** and **`500,000+` `closure.callee.reject`** events (`74,954` on `context.WithTimeout`, `55,446` on `(*Session).beginOperation`, `36,768` on `go/types.Unalias`, `25,361` on `gofresh.analysisBound`, `16,877` on `pew.commandContext`, `15,863` on `(reflect.Value).Type`).
- **Root Cause**:
  1. In `newFreshParamAnalysis` (`internal/closure/freshparam.go:494-501`):
     ```go
     if len(reach.enumeratedRootSites) > 0 || len(reach.enumeratedFieldStores) > 0 || reach.enumeratedRoot {
         fp.enumRoot = reach.subjectRoot
         ...
     }
     ```
     Whenever a subject widens (`reach.enumeratedRoot == true`, which holds for all 273 test subjects in `pew/cmd/pew` due to `(*openpgp/packet.Config).Now`), `fp.enumRoot` is set to `reach.subjectRoot` (`!= nil`) while `fp.enumOnly` is `false`.
  2. In `dynamic_walk.go:249-250` and `dispatch_targets.go:120`:
     ```go
     inCycleScope := (fp.enumOnly || fp.enumRoot == nil) && (fp.inProgramSites || fp.inFieldStore || fp.enumOnly) && value.Parent() != fp.enumRoot && value.Parent() != fp.subjectRoot
     inProgScope := outsideCallee && inCycleScope && !fp.ignoreContextValuePayload && fp.enumRecvNamed == nil
     ```
     Because `fp.enumOnly` is `false` and `fp.enumRoot != nil`, **`(fp.enumOnly || fp.enumRoot == nil)` is `false` for the entire subject walk**!
  3. Consequently, **`inProgScope` is `false` for 100% of all widened subjects**, completely disabling `summary.progClosedDone`, `summary.progCarrierDone`, `summary.progInvokeTargets`, `summary.progInvokeFailed`, `summary.progTypesMemo`, `summary.progTypesFailed`, `summary.progFuncsMemo`, and `summary.progFuncsFailed` across all 273 subjects! Every subject re-walks the entire 462-package SSA program (`gofresh`, `go-x-tools`, `go-git`, `ProtonMail/go-crypto`, `golang.org/x/crypto/ssh`) from scratch!
  4. Furthermore, why did `inCycleScope` / `inProgScope` check `(fp.enumOnly || fp.enumRoot == nil)` and `fp.enumRecvNamed == nil`?
     - When `fp.inProgramSites || fp.inFieldStore` is true (i.e., we have already crossed into whole-program callers `withProgramSites` or whole-program struct field stores) AND `!myUsedSubject` (the sub-walk never touched `fp.enumRoot` or `fp.subjectRoot`), the result is 100% independent of `fp.enumRoot` and `fp.enumRecvNamed` (as long as `fp.enumRecvNamed` was not matched, which is already tracked by `*fp.usedSubject = true` at `callee_return.go:309`, `dynamic_walk.go:126,245,511,1364,1579`)!

#### Issue B (`gofresh/internal/closure`): `contextCancelMethodFP` / `withIgnoreContextValuePayload` Wipes All Memos (`resetDynamicMemos()`) and Disables `inProgScope` on Every `ctx.Err()` / `ctx.Done()` / `ctx.Deadline()` Site (`dispatch_targets.go:118-120, 952-974`, `callee_return.go:66, 146`)
- **Evidence from `/tmp/pew-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  - Within a single subject walk, `compareObservations -> (context.Context).Err` failed `8,145` times (`~30` times per subject), `(*Snapshot).ensureObservable -> (context.Context).Err` failed `4,770` times, `movedBracketRoot -> (context.Context).Err` failed `4,212` times, and `(*Session).beginOperation` failed `55,446` times (`~203` times per subject).
- **Root Cause**:
  1. In `closedInvokeTargets` (`dispatch_targets.go:118`):
     `fp = contextCancelMethodFP(site, fp)` is called **before** checking `pFailed[site]` / `pTargets[site]` and `fp.invokeFailed[site]` / `fp.invokeTargets[site]`.
  2. `withIgnoreContextValuePayload(fp)` (`dispatch_targets.go:952-967`) creates a local copy `copied := *fp` and calls **`copied.resetDynamicMemos()`**, allocating brand-new empty maps for `invokeTargets`, `invokeFailed`, `closedDone`, `carrierDone`, `typesMemo`, `typesFailed`, `funcsMemo`, `funcsFailed`.
  3. Because `copied` has `ignoreContextValuePayload == true` and brand-new empty maps:
     - `inProgScope` (`!fp.ignoreContextValuePayload`) is forced to `false`.
     - `fp.invokeFailed[site]` / `fp.invokeTargets[site]` are always empty on entry to `closedInvokeTargets` for every `ctx.Err()` call, and any entries written at exit (`lines 167, 194`) are written to the throwaway local `copied` and immediately discarded when `closedInvokeTargets` returns!
     - Inside the `ctx.Err()` receiver walk, all work done in `copied.closedDone` and `copied.carrierDone` is also discarded when `closedInvokeTargets` returns, so the next `ctx.Err()` call in the same function/subject re-walks the entire `context.Context` call chain from scratch!

#### Issue C (`gofresh/internal/closure`): `(*Session).beginOperation(ctx)` Rejected on `tupleIndex == 0` (`gate: "struct_arg_walk"`) Because Tuple-Return Closure at `tupleIndex == 1` Captures `s *Session` (`callee_return.go:288-303`, `attribution_harness.go:279-348`)
- **Evidence from `/tmp/pew-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  - `(*github.com/greatliontech/gofresh.Session).beginOperation` is rejected `55,446` times on `tuple_index: 0` with `gate: "struct_arg_walk"`, which then causes `compareObservations`, `ensureObservable`, `movedBracketRoot`, `verifyToolchainSurfaceAndDelegatesWithIO`, and `verifyListedPackageWithIOAndSalt` to fail `closedInvokeTargets` on `(context.Context).Err` (`eval_recv`).
- **Root Cause**:
  - `(*Session).beginOperation(ctx context.Context) (context.Context, func(*error))` (`gofresh/progress.go:89-113`) returns `(ctx, finish)`:
    - Result `0` (`tupleIndex == 0`) is `ctx` or `context.WithValue(ctx, opAccountKey{}, acc)`. Receiver `s *Session` only has a carrier-free nil check `if s.progress == nil` on the path to result `0`.
    - Result `1` (`tupleIndex == 1`) is a closure `func(err *error)` (`*ssa.MakeClosure`) that captures `s *Session` and is returned directly at `ret.Results[1]`.
  - When `calleeResultDynamicValue` evaluates `beginOperation` for **`tupleIndex == 0`** (`context.Context`), `paramMaySupplyDynamicSeen` (`attribution_harness.go:279-348`) and `calleeParamMayAffectResult` (`callee_return.go:616-721`) inspect all referrers of `s` (`callee.Params[0]`) without considering `tupleIndex`. Because `s` is captured by `*ssa.MakeClosure` (even though that `*ssa.MakeClosure`'s only referrer in `beginOperation` is `*ssa.Return` at `Results[1]`, never `Results[0]` and never called inside `beginOperation`), `paramMaySupplyDynamicSeen` returns `true` and requires walking `s *Session` (`struct_arg_walk`), which fails because `Session` holds unclosed fields.

#### Issue D (`gofresh/scan.go`, `gofresh/view.go`, `stipulator/internal/backends/golang`): 6x `dynstate.FactOf` Recomputation & Unscoped `dischargeUnreachableCulprits`
- **Evidence from `/tmp/pew-full-check-p1.jsonl` & `/tmp/gofresh-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  1. In `pew` Pass 1, `dynstate.FactOf` ran `637` times across `213` unique packages (`110.29s` total). In `gofresh` Pass 1 (`3m36.8s` discovery), `dynstate.FactOf` ran **`6` times** on `gofresh/internal/closure` (`105,414.9 ms` = `1m45s`, `~17.5s` each) and **`6` times** on `gofresh` (`20,922.4 ms` = `20.9s`, `~3.5s` each), totaling **`126.3s` (`2m06s`)**:
     - `stipulator check` opened 3 separate sessions with 3 distinct `guards_buildconfig` / `scope` keys:
       1. `resolve:new_served`: `build_flags: []` (`GeneralExecution`), `guards_buildconfig: b4444cb9c8832c67ca6b647d3b4697f4` -> runs `observeSnapshot` (`2` passes of `observeView`).
       2. `resolve:opening_capture`: `build_flags: ["-tags=race"]` (`GeneralExecution`), `guards_buildconfig: c67f40b984cc6f1200cb27fb8632d770` -> runs `observeSnapshot` (`2` passes of `observeView`).
       3. `witness:group_snapshot`: `build_flags: ["-race"]` (`PackageExecution`), `guards_buildconfig: 2a6de3e57d43ffc81c8c93fcbdd46261` -> runs `observeSnapshot` (`2` passes of `observeView`).
     - `scope.Facts()` (`internal/toolchain/toolchain.go`) includes `|package-process-execution` when `PackageProcess == true`, causing `dynamicstate` disk cache misses between `GeneralExecution` and `PackageExecution`, even though `dynstate.FactOf(audited, pkg, singleSubject)` does not depend on `packageProcess` (`packageProcess` only affects `dynstate.Compose`).
     - Similarly, `SingleSubjectExecution` (`soloSession`) misses the `dynamicstate` cache again because `singleSubject` gates `SingleSubjectSites` population in `FactOf`. Always populating `SingleSubjectSites` in `FactOf` makes `dynstate.Fact` independent of both `PackageProcess` and `SingleSubjectExecution`.
     - For `MutableLocalPackage` packages (`gofresh`, `gofresh/internal/closure`, `pew/cmd/pew`), `observeSnapshot` calls `observeView` twice (`first` and `second`) and skips disk/session memoization in `scanViewSubjectsWithMemo` (`scan.go`), re-running `dynstate.FactOf` twice per snapshot even when the package's source file digests did not change between `first` and `second`!
  2. In `scanViewSubjectsWithMemo` (`gofresh/scan.go:161-168`), `dischargeUnreachableCulprits` (`scan.go:463-506`) iterates over `for subject := range scan.known` (**all 616 declared symbols** in `cmd/pew`, running 10 batches of 64 subjects = **`4m40.6s`**), AND inside `provenanceBFSWithCandidates` (`attribution_provenance.go:1860`), `getProvFP()` calls `fp.resetDynamicMemos()` on **every single subject (`616` times)** instead of sharing the non-subject-dependent memos across subjects in the batch.

#### Issue E (`pew` & `stipulator` Post-Run Validation Uncacheable Witness Causes — `465/480` Uncacheable in `pew` Pass 1)
- **Evidence from `task-31210.log` (`pew` Pass 1)**:
  - Out of `480` executed test subjects in `pew`, **`465` were marked `uncacheable`** in Pass 1 (causing Pass 2 warm `stipulator check` to re-execute `465/480` subjects across all 13 packages!):
    1. **`272` subjects (`cmd/pew`)**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: external directory input: /home/nikolas/.cache/gofresh` (and `ProtonMail/go-crypto/openpgp/internal/algorithm.CipherById escapes writable`).
       - Why did `cmd/pew` tests access `/home/nikolas/.cache/gofresh`? Because tests in `cmd/pew` invoke `pew` -> `gofresh` without isolating `XDG_CACHE_HOME` (or `gofresh`'s own cache root `~/.cache/gofresh` is not recognized as an internal/scratch cache root when `pew` tests run `gofresh`).
    2. **`55 + 38 = 93` subjects**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: runtime input not covered by observation bracket: /tmp/stipulator-retained-530510267/package.test` and `/tmp/stipulator-retained-689746490/package.test`.
       - `stipulator` builds the retained test binary in `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<random>/package.test`. When tests read their own executable path (`os.Executable()`), `testlog` records `open /tmp/stipulator-retained-.../package.test` or `stat /tmp/stipulator-retained-.../package.test`, which is outside the package's observation bracket!
    3. **`43` subjects**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: reaches go:linkname (opaque linkage)`.
    4. **`17` subjects**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: runtime input not covered by observation bracket: /usr/bin/git`.
    5. **`11` subjects**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: package graph shares mutated dynamic state: github.com/google/pprof/profile: github.com/google/pprof/profile.functionDecoder escapes writable`.
    6. **`11` subjects**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: runtime input not covered by observation bracket: internal`.
    7. **`5` subjects**: `uncacheable: post-run validation: reaches testing.Setenv (process or path mutation)`.
    8. **Complete per-package uncacheable breakdown (`publish_package` + `observability` + `tier2.subject.done` in `/tmp/pew-full-check-p1.jsonl`)**:
       - **`github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/profiles` (`14` subjects), `internal/recordingtest` (`2` subjects), `internal/run` (`55/56` subjects), `internal/store` (`38/39` subjects)**:
         - Every single subject in these 4 packages (`110` test subjects / `220` observability evaluations) had **`observable: false, reason: "startup effect: reaches unaudited standard operation math.Inf"`**!
         - Because `math` (`math.Inf`, `math.NaN`, `math.IsNaN`, `math.IsInf`, `math.Abs`, `math.Sqrt`, `math.Log`, `math.Exp`, etc.) is not in the audited standard-library surface (`internal/auditset`), a package-level `var ... = math.Inf(...)` initializer poisons startup observability for the entire package subgraph (`observable = false`), preventing Tier-2 per-subject effect narrowing and causing `internal/profiles` (`3`), `internal/recordingtest` (`2`), `internal/run` (`1`), and `internal/store` (`1`) to fall back to Tier-1 `maximal` (`reaches go:linkname (opaque linkage)` from `golang.org/x/sys/unix`).
       - **`github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/compare` (`36` subjects) & `internal/guidance` (`1` subject)**:
         - `tier2.subject.done` reported `reason: "reaches fmt.Fprintf (formatted output)"` because `fmt.Fprint` / `fmt.Fprintf` / `fmt.Fprintln` (which write to their `w io.Writer` first argument, such as `&bytes.Buffer{}` or `&strings.Builder{}` or `sha256.New()`, unlike `fmt.Printf` which writes to `os.Stdout`) are unconditionally classified as `EffectFormattedOutput` (`Class B`) instead of checking whether `w` (`arg 0`) is a closed in-memory `io.Writer`.
         - `observability` on `internal/compare` also reported `startup effect: reaches unaudited standard operation hash/maphash.MakeSeed` (from `golang.org/x/perf/benchproc` `init()`), causing fallback to Tier-1 `reaches go:linkname (opaque linkage)`.
       - **`github.com/greatliontech/pew` (`1` subject) & `internal/shortgates` (`3` subjects)**:
         - `observability` reported `subject reachability is not closed: interface invoke outside RTA: github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/shortgates.check$2 dispatches go/ast.Stmt.Pos` (invoking `.Pos()` on an `ast.Stmt` node produced by `go/parser.ParseFile`).
       - **`github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/guidance` (`TestMustRefusalsNameTheTool`)**:
         - `15/17` subjects in `internal/guidance` were `observable=true, effect_free=true` and cached (`15 records`). `TestMustRefusalsNameTheTool` failed `observability` with `computed function call in github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/guidance.TestMustRefusalsNameTheTool$1 calling parameter f` (a local `mustPanic := func(f func())` closure called on two inline function literals).
       - **`github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/toolversion` (`3` subjects)**:
         - `observability` reported `package scan: reaches unaudited standard operation runtime/debug.BuildInfo` / `runtime/debug.ReadBuildInfo`.
       - **`github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/trace` (`5` subjects)**:
         - `2` subjects failed `observability` with `interface invoke outside RTA: github.com/greatliontech/pew/internal/trace.emitSlow dispatches context.Context.Value` / `WithFilteredSink dispatches context.Context.Value` / `emitSlow dispatches Sink.Emit`.

#### Issue F (`gofresh/internal/runtimeinput/testlog.go` & `stipulator/internal/backends/golang/nativeexecution.go`): Multi-Hundred-MiB `testlog.txt` Allocation/Parsing, Leaked `/tmp` Artifacts, and `unit.Close` Bottlenecks
- **Evidence from `/tmp/pew-full-check-p1.jsonl` & `/tmp/gofresh-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  1. During `gofresh` Pass 1:
     - `gofresh` `package.test` generated a **`957 MiB` (`8,487,316` lines)** `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-2429520621.txt` (`testlog.extract.done`: `10,837 ms` = `10.8s`, `path_count: 345`, `env_count: 18`).
     - `gofresh/internal/closure` `package.test` generated a **`343 MiB` (`3,943,805` lines)** `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-3721028677.txt` (`testlog.extract.done`: `3,962 ms` = `4.0s`, `path_count: 2`, `env_count: 5`).
     - `fromTestLog` (`internal/runtimeinput/testlog.go:342-350`) reads the entire `957 MiB` file into memory (`os.ReadFile`), calls `bytes.Split(log, []byte{'\n'})` (allocating an `8.5M`-element `[][]byte` slice = `204 MiB` of slice headers on top of the `957 MiB` buffer, driving peak RSS to `3.9 GiB`), and runs `applyTestLogLine` (`filepath.Clean` + map lookups) `8.5M` times without streaming `bufio.Scanner` or deduplicating `(cwd, op, arg)` lines before path resolution.
  2. `/tmp` has **`487` leaked `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-*.txt` files** and **`2,974` leaked `/tmp/gofresh-inmem-pkg-*` directories** because `stipulator` (`nativeexecution.go:355`) creates `os.CreateTemp("", "stipulator-testlog-*.txt")` and never removes it after `unit.Close`.
  3. On `pew/cmd/pew` (`18:33:51`), `testlog.extract.done` took **`26.3s`** (`path_count: 5950, unverifiable: true`), and `unit.Close` (`Validate` + `Check`) performed `1,908` `cache.load` and `1,596` `closure.memo.load` disk reads (`10s`), while `~/.cache/gofresh` hit 4 `cache.prune` evictions (`2 GiB` cap).

#### Issue G (`gofresh/unit.go`, `gofresh/judgment.go`, `stipulator/internal/backends/golang/nativeexecution.go`, `retainedbuild.go`): Ephemeral `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` Poisons `RuntimeInputs` and Causes `693/714` `race:core` Witnesses in `gofresh` Pass 2 to Re-Execute!
- **Evidence from `/tmp/gofresh-full-check-p2.jsonl`**:
  - In `gofresh` Pass 2 (`=== GOFRESH PASS 2 ===`), `401/404` `race:leaf` witnesses served fresh from cache, and `20/20` solo `Fuzz*` witnesses in `race:core` served fresh from cache, **but `693/694` batch subjects in `race:core` (`361` in `gofresh`, `332` in `gofresh/internal/closure`) missed the witness cache and re-executed (`18m56s`)**!
  - `session:check_verdict` in `/tmp/gofresh-full-check-p2.jsonl` proves the exact cause:
    - `361` subjects in `gofresh`: `status: "stale", reason: "runtimeinputs (moved: path /tmp/stipulator-retained-4188518620/package.test)"`
    - `332` subjects in `gofresh/internal/closure`: `status: "stale", reason: "runtimeinputs (moved: path /tmp/stipulator-retained-4015067910/package.test)"`
- **Three-Part Root Cause**:
  1. **`stipulator` Retained Test Binary Path Not Excluded from `ProcessDescription` (`retainedbuild.go:51, 95`, `nativeexecution.go:286-289`)**:
     - `stipulator` builds each package's retained test binary in `os.MkdirTemp("", "stipulator-retained-*")` (`retainedbuild.go:95`) and deletes that temporary directory in `retainedArtifact.Close()` (`retainedbuild.go:51`) as soon as the package finishes executing.
     - During execution of `package.test`, the Go test binary (or `os.Executable()` / stack/symbol/DWARF inspection) stats/opens `/proc/self/exe` -> `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test`, which `GODEBUG=testlogfile` records.
     - `prepareNativeAttempt` (`nativeexecution.go:286-289`) passes `ExcludePaths: leg.excludePaths` to `gofresh.ProcessDescription`, which does NOT include `artifact.Path` (or its parent `stipulator-retained-*` temp dir)!
  2. **`gofresh` Attaches Unverifiable `RuntimeInputs` to Pure (`assume_pure: true`) Subjects and Discharges `rt.Unverifiable` at Publish Time Only to Fail on `rt.BracketMoved` After Temp Dir Deletion (`unit.go:475-504`, `judgment.go:338-375`)**:
     - In `gofresh`, `.stipulator/policy.textproto` declares `assume_pure: true`, so `PureSubjects` includes all test subjects and `len(x.observed) == 0`.
     - However, in `Unit.Close` (`unit.go:475-504`), because `x.process.Capture != ""` (`testlog`), `x.frame.ObserveInputs` still extracts `testlog.txt`, sees `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` outside the observation bracket (`rt.Unverifiable == true`), and attaches `state.Manifest` (containing `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test`) to `Fingerprint.RuntimeInputs` for all `361` (`gofresh`) and `332` (`internal/closure`) batch subjects!
     - During `Unit.Close`'s self-check (`decideAfterClosureObserved`, `judgment.go:338-375`), `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` **still exists on disk** (`rt.BracketMoved == false`, `rt.Unverifiable == true`), and `if pure.accepted() { return Verdict{Status: Valid} }` (`line 369`) discharges `rt.Unverifiable`, allowing `publishPackage` to persist all `693` records with `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` baked into `Fingerprint.RuntimeInputs`!
     - Immediately after `publishPackage`, `retainedArtifact.Close()` deletes `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test`.
     - On Pass 2, `decideKnownGuards` (`judgment.go:315-318`) checks `if rt.BracketMoved` **before** `decideAfterClosureObserved` checks `if pure.accepted()`. Because `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` no longer exists (`ENOENT`), `rt.BracketMoved` is `true`, and all `693` records are rejected as `Stale` (`runtimeinputs (moved: path /tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test)`)!
  3. **Misleading `re-executed: closure` Attribution (`witnessrun.go:998, 1022`) & Unpruned Stale Witness Cache Records (`stipulator`)**:
     - In `prepareWitnessGroups` (`stipulator/internal/backends/golang/witnessrun.go:1011-1049`), round-based variant checking orders `groupCached` with the **most recently installed record first (`round == 0`)** and older historical records in `round == 1, 2, ...`.
     - However, in `checkRound` (`witnessrun.go:998-1001`) and `roundCandidates` (`witnessrun.go:1022`), every failed round unconditionally overwrites `wg.executedWhy[s] = verdictExecutedReason(checked.Verdict.Reason)`!
     - Consequently, when all variants fail, `wg.executedWhy[s]` retains the refusal reason of the **oldest historical variant (`round == len-1`)** (`closure` from weeks ago) instead of the **most recent variant (`round == 0`)** (`runtimeinputs (moved: path /tmp/stipulator-retained-.../package.test)`). Only the `3` newly added tests that had a single variant (`round == 0` only) reported `runtimeinputs (moved: ...)`, while the other `690` tests had their `round == 0` `runtimeinputs` reason overwritten by `round > 0`'s old `closure` refusal!
     - Furthermore, `witnesscache` never prunes superseded per-subject records (`4,508` observed refusals on `manifest version 2`, plus multiple stale records per subject).

#### Issue H (`stipulator/internal/backends/golang/served.go` & `selection.go`): `reuseCheckedCapture` Fails on Non-Callable `ClosureStrategy` Symbols and `Fuzz*` Targets Force 20 Solo Invocations
- **Evidence from `/tmp/gofresh-full-check-p2.jsonl`**:
  1. In `gofresh` Pass 2 discovery (`1m20.4s`), `resolve:new_served` took `34.1s` (`1,200` subjects) and `resolve:opening_capture` took another `32.8s` (`1,200` subjects) even though `serveSelection` had just computed the exact same `1,200` fingerprints!
     - In `served.go:480-505, 634-637`: `serveSelection` partitions non-callable symbols (`gofresh.ClosureStrategy`, `gofresh.RecordedBatchError`, where `rec.Fingerprint == (gofresh.Fingerprint{})`) into `nonCallables` and never records them in `s.checkedCaptures[key]` or `s.checkedUnknown[key]`.
     - When `openingCapture` calls `s.reuseCheckedCapture(key, s.unserved)` (`line 455`), it iterates over `s.unserved`, hits `gofresh.ClosureStrategy` first (`line 501`), sees it in neither `checked` nor `unknown`, and returns `false` — discarding all `1,198` callable fingerprints and re-running `engine.Snapshot` + `CaptureBatch` twice!
  2. In `selection.go`, `20` `Fuzz*` functions (`10` in `gofresh`, `10` in `gofresh/internal/closure`) are split out of batch execution (`cause: "mixed_fuzz"`) into `20` separate solo `package.test` invocations in Pass 1 (`~1.5s-3s` each = `~45s` overhead).

#### Issue I (`gofresh/validate.go:475-485` & `gofresh/checking.go:523-542`): Unmemoized `withMovedInputs` Amplification & `CheckingUnit.Close` Re-Evaluating Already-`Stale` Slots (`34.3s` in `phase verification`)
- **Evidence from `/tmp/gofresh-full-check-p2.jsonl`**:
  - In `gofresh` Pass 2 `phase verification` (`34.3s`), `witness:revalidate_served` took **`17,825 ms` (`17.8s`)** on `github.com/greatliontech/gofresh` (to revalidate `10` served solo fuzz records!) and **`13,246 ms` (`13.2s`)** on `github.com/greatliontech/gofresh/internal/closure` (to revalidate `10` served solo fuzz records!).
- **Two-Part Root Cause**:
  1. In `withMovedInputs` (`validate.go:475-485`), `movedInputsForView(ctx, v, recorded.RuntimeInputs)` has **no per-manifest memoization**. When `361` records in `gofresh` and `332` records in `gofresh/internal/closure` share the same `recorded.RuntimeInputs` manifest (`345` paths in `gofresh`), `withMovedInputs` calls `movedInputsForView` `361` times during `CheckBatch` (`selectPkg`) and `361` times again during `CheckingUnit.Close` (`revalidateServed`), re-decoding the manifest and re-statting/re-hashing all `345` paths `361 * 345 = 124,545` times per call!
  2. In `CheckingUnit.close` (`checking.go:523-542`), `u.slots` contains all `1,451` historical candidates checked during `selectPkg` (`1,431` of which were already judged `Stale` in `CheckBatch`). Line 525 only sets `records[i].refusal = slot.result.verdict` when `!slot.result.evaluated`, so all `1,431` already-`Stale` evaluated slots are re-checked (`observeRecordInputs` + `withMovedInputs`) in `current.checkRecords` (`line 529`), only for lines 537-538 (`if slot.result.verdict.Status == Stale { result.verdict = slot.result.verdict }`) to overwrite and discard the recomputed verdict!

#### Issue J (`gofresh/internal/dynstate/summary_leakfree.go:1243`, `gofresh/internal/closure/rooted.go:139`, `attribution_provenance.go:1860-1885`): `sync.Once` Memoizer Disqualification (`embeddedGuidance is mutated` Poisoning 996/1069 Subjects) & `ComputeRootedFunctions` Running Without `progSummary`
- **Evidence from `/tmp/gomutant-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  - In `gomutant` Pass 1 discovery (`8m42.5s`), `witness:capture_native_generation` took **`485,878 ms` (`8m05.8s`)** because **`996` out of `1,069` test subjects** failed `CheckBatch` with `SharedDynamicStateCause` (`github.com/greatliontech/gomutant.embeddedGuidance is mutated`), forcing `soloSession` (`SingleSubjectExecution`) to run `dischargeUnreachableCulprits` -> `ComputeRootedFunctions` across all `1,069` declared symbols!
- **Two-Part Root Cause**:
  1. **`sync.Once` Struct-Pointer Memoizer Disqualified Because `onceFill` omits `consume(lhs)` (`internal/dynstate/summary_leakfree.go:1243-1245, 1450-1460`)**:
     - `var embeddedGuidance = guidance.Embed("gomutant", guidanceSrc)` (`gomutant/guidancedoc.go:22`) is a `*guidance.Embedded` whose method `(*Embedded).Document()` (`internal/guidance/guidance.go:752-755`) runs:
       ```go
       func (e *Embedded) Document() (*Document, error) {
           e.once.Do(func() { e.doc, e.err = Parse(e.src) })
           return e.doc, e.err
       }
       ```
     - In `summary_leakfree.go:1131-1142`, `onceFill[stmt] = true` recognizes `e.doc, e.err = Parse(e.src)` inside `e.once.Do(func() { ... })`.
     - At `*ast.AssignStmt` (`lines 1243-1245`), when `recvRooted(lhs)` and `onceFill[n]` is `true`, the code avoids setting `disqualified[key] = true`, **but forgets to call `consume(lhs)`**!
     - When `ast.Inspect` subsequently visits the child `*ast.SelectorExpr` `e.doc` (`*Document`) and `e.err` (`error`) on the LHS of the assignment (`lines 1450-1460`), `!allowed[syntaxutil.RootIdent(n.X)]` is `true`, and `refconfine.CallResultHandsOut(p.TypesInfo.TypeOf(n))` returns `true` (because `*Document` is a pointer and `error` is an interface), setting `disqualified["Embedded.Document"] = true` (and similarly for `e.src` `[]byte` passed to `Parse(e.src)`)!
     - That causes `(*Embedded).Document` to be marked as mutating its receiver, which marks `embeddedGuidance` as `is mutated` and poisons all `996` subjects in `gomutant`, `internal/cmd`, `internal/mcpserver`, and `cmd/gomutant`!
  2. **`ComputeRootedFunctions` (`rooted.go:139`) Never Calls `getSummary(ctx, audited, pureDelegates, prog)` and `getProvFP()` (`attribution_provenance.go:1860-1885`) Resets Memos on Every Subject**:
     - In `rootedGroup` (`rooted.go:139`), `attributedReachableSets` is called on `prog` without first initializing `getSummary(ctx, h.SelectionAudited(), h.source.pureDelegates, prog)`.
     - Inside `provenanceBFSWithCandidates` (`attribution_provenance.go:1865-1885`), `sum := lookupSummaryForProg(prog, audited)` therefore returns **`nil`**, leaving `fp.summary = nil`, `fp.inProgramSites = false`, `fp.programSites = nil`, and `fp.programValueRef = nil`!
     - Worse, `getProvFP()` is local to `provenanceBFSWithCandidates` (called 3 times per subject across `1,069` subjects = `3,207` calls) and calls `fp.resetDynamicMemos()` on every call, even though `getProvFP()` has `fp.subjectRoot == nil` and `fp.enumRoot == nil` (100% subject-independent!).

#### Issue K (`gofresh/internal/runtimeinput/testlog.go` & `stipulator/internal/backends/golang/retainedenv.go`, `nativeexecution.go`): `chdir` Elision Omission (`t.Chdir(t.TempDir())`), Sticky `cwdChanged` Across Restored `t.Chdir`, and Missing `ScratchRoot` / Delayed `logPath` Cleanup in `stipulator`
- **Evidence from `/tmp/gomutant-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  1. **`134` subjects in `gomutant/internal/cmd` failed post-run validation with `runtime input not covered by observation bracket: /tmp/TestVersionSurfaces3187139984/001`**:
     - `TestVersionSurfaces` (`gomutant/internal/cmd/version_test.go:22-26`) creates `dir := t.TempDir()` and calls `t.Chdir(dir)`.
     - In `fromTestLog` (`gofresh/internal/runtimeinput/testlog.go:392, 417, 486-507`), `case "open":` and `case "stat":` both check `if !ambiguousParent && !relativeAfterChdir && classifier.elidesUnambiguous(p) { continue }`, which elides `testing.T.TempDir()` paths (`testTempRoot`).
     - However, `case "chdir":` (`lines 493-507`) **never calls `classifier.elidesUnambiguous(p)`**! Instead it calls `classifier.classify(p)` and inserts `/tmp/TestVersionSurfaces3187139984/001` into `m.Paths` (`line 501`), which then fails `coverage.covers(id)` (`line 549`) with `runtime input not covered by observation bracket: /tmp/TestVersionSurfaces3187139984/001`!
  2. **`552` subjects in `github.com/greatliontech/gomutant` failed post-run validation with `relative runtime input after working-directory change: analysisbudget_test.go`**:
     - When a test in `github.com/greatliontech/gomutant` calls `t.Chdir(dir)`, `testlog.go:508-509` sets `cwd = p` and `cwdChanged = true`.
     - When `t.Chdir`'s cleanup runs at the end of that test, `os.Chdir(packageDir)` logs `chdir /home/nikolas/repos/github.com/greatliontech/gomutant`, restoring `cwd` to `packageDir`, **but `cwdChanged` remains `true` for all subsequent tests in the batch**!
     - Any subsequent test in the batch that opens/stats a relative path (like `"analysisbudget_test.go"`) while `cwd == packageDir` hits `relativeAfterChdir := cwdChanged && !filepath.IsAbs(name)` (`lines 386, 410-412`) and fails with `"relative runtime input after working-directory change: analysisbudget_test.go"`.
  3. **`stipulator` Omits `ScratchRoot` on `ProcessDescription` (`retainedenv.go:28-64`, `nativeexecution.go:286-289`) and Delays `logPath` Removal Until Whole-Invocation Exit (`nativeexecution.go:58-90`, `execute.go:313-317, 548`)**:
     - Unlike `gomutant` (which allocates a per-execution `ScratchRoot`, sets `TMPDIR` in the child environment, and passes `ScratchRoot` on `gofresh.ProcessDescription`), `stipulator` leaves `ScratchRoot: ""` and `TMPDIR` at `/tmp`. As a result, `os.CreateTemp("", ...)` files created by tests or child `go` commands are not elided under `ScratchRoot`, and leaked temp files accumulate in `/tmp` (`tmpfs` RAM — `35 GiB` used in `/tmp`!).
     - In `finishNativeRun` (`nativeexecution.go:58-90`), after `closeNativeProcess(ctx, a.unit)` finishes reading `a.logPath`, `a.logPath` (`2.1 GiB` for `gomutant`, `957 MiB` for `gofresh`) is NOT removed immediately — it is retained in `/tmp` (`tmpfs` RAM) until `executeSelection` exits (`execute.go:548`)!

#### Issue L (`stipulator/internal/backends/golang/resolutioncontext.go`, `served.go` & `gofresh/internal/closure/listingmemo.go`): Workspace `buildSelection` 6x Whole-Corpus Amplification (`2m13.8s` in `openingCapture`) & Poisoned `listingmemo` `rec.Absent` When `h.dir` Is a Nested Submodule (`217` `go list` Spawns)
- **Evidence from `/tmp/stipulator-full-check-p1.jsonl`**:
  1. **`stipulator` `openingCapture` Evaluates All `1,264` Corpus Symbols Across 6 `buildSelection`s (`15,168` Subject Evaluations, `133.8s`) Even Though 100% (`1,268/1,268`) Resolve Under `"default"`**:
     - `stipulator/.stipulator/policy.textproto` declares `race` (`module_root: ""`), `race:stipulate` (`module_root: "stipulate"`), and `race:stipulate/structural` (`module_root: "stipulate/structural"`).
     - `policyBuildSelections` (`golang.go:533-549`) generates 6 `buildSelection`s: `default`, `module=stipulate`, `module=stipulate/structural`, `tags=race`, `module=stipulate,tags=race`, `module=stipulate/structural,tags=race`.
     - In `buildSelection.members(root)` (`resolutioncontext.go:36-38`), when `go.work` exists at `root` (`hasWork == true`) and `workspaceMode != OFF`, `sel.members(root)` returns `workspaceMembers(root)` (`[".", "stipulate", "stipulate/structural"]`) **ignoring `sel.moduleRoot`**!
     - Consequently:
       - `declarationPackages` (`packageinventory.go:56-84`) marks all `1,264` symbols across `.`, `stipulate`, and `stipulate/structural` as belonging to `s.inventory[key]` for **every one of the 6 selections**.
       - `openingCapture` (`served.go:453-466`) eagerly calls `s.captureUnder(endpoint, key, s.unserved, "opening")` on all `1,264` symbols for all 6 selections BEFORE `typed_load` runs — even though `"default"` already loads all 3 workspace members (`".", "stipulate", "stipulate/structural"`) and resolves **100% (`1,268/1,268`) of the symbols**, so the other 5 selections resolve **0 symbols**!
       - Even when a secondary selection is needed for `//go:build` tag-split symbols, `openingCapture` does not need to pre-capture all `1,268` symbols under every secondary selection before knowing which symbols failed to resolve in earlier selections (or when `sel.moduleRoot != ""` under an active `go.work` produces an identical workspace member set).
  2. **`gofresh/internal/closure/listingmemo.go:360-370` Records Existing Nested `go.mod` Files in `rec.Absent` When `h.dir` Is a Nested Workspace Submodule**:
     - When `h.dir` is `/home/nikolas/repos/github.com/greatliontech/stipulator/stipulate/structural` (under `go.work`) and `gofresh` lists a package in the root module (`github.com/greatliontech/stipulator/internal/...`), that package's dependency cone `pkgs` does not contain any package from `stipulate/structural`.
     - Therefore, `mainDirs` (`listingmemo.go:304-312`) only contains `/home/nikolas/repos/github.com/greatliontech/stipulator` (not `/stipulator/stipulate/structural`).
     - At lines 360-364, `strings.HasPrefix(dir, candidate+"/")` matches `mainDir = "/home/nikolas/repos/github.com/greatliontech/stipulator"`, and then lines 368-370 walk `for d := dir; d != mainDir; d = filepath.Dir(d)` and set:
       `absent["/home/nikolas/repos/github.com/greatliontech/stipulator/stipulate/structural/go.mod"] = true`
       `absent["/home/nikolas/repos/github.com/greatliontech/stipulator/stipulate/go.mod"] = true`
       even though both `go.mod` files **exist on disk**!
     - `storeListing` writes this poisoned `rec.Absent` to `~/.cache/gofresh/listing/...`, and on the very next pass `loadListing` (`lines 163-166`) checks `os.Lstat(path); !os.IsNotExist(err)` -> returns `false`, causing **100% `loadListing` validation misses** and spawning `go list` `217` times (`22.2s`) in `stipulate/structural` and `105` times (`10.4s`) in `stipulate`!

#### Issue M (`stipulator/internal/backends/golang/admission.go:489-520`, `gofresh/internal/runtimeinput/scratch.go:393-395`, `testlog.go:911-936`): `GOMAXPROCS=2` Long-Pole Starvation, `ephemeralScratch` Ordering Against `retainedArtifact.Close()`, and Unisolated Cache / Volatile `/proc` Reads in `stipulator/internal/backends/golang`
- **Evidence from `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-447714370.txt` & `/proc/609483/environ`**:
  1. **`witnessSpawnBound` / `witnessChildWidth` (`admission.go:489-520`, `observe.go:173-181`) Starves Long-Pole Packages at `GOMAXPROCS=2` on a 32-Thread Host**:
     - On a 32-processor machine (`processors = 32`), `witnessSpawnBound(32, packages)` sets `n.SpawnBound = min(16, packages)` (`16` in `stipulator`'s 36-package `race`, `16` in `gomutant`'s 17-package suite, `13` in `pew`'s 13-package suite), and `witnessChildWidth(n) = 32 / SpawnBound = 2` (`GOMAXPROCS=2` in `n.WitnessEnv`).
     - In all three repositories, the 12-35 small leaf packages finish within the first 30-120 seconds, leaving a single heavy integration package (`stipulator/internal/backends/golang` with `719` sequential `Test*` functions and `0` `t.Parallel()` calls, `gomutant` + `gomutant/internal/cmd`, `pew/cmd/pew`) running alone for 15-40 minutes with `GOMAXPROCS=2` while 30 of 32 CPU threads sit idle.
     - Worse, because `GOMAXPROCS=2` is exported into `package.test`'s environment, every child `go test -c -race`, `go list`, and `gofresh` SSA/RTA build spawned inside those integration tests inherits `GOMAXPROCS=2`.
  2. **Why `ephemeralScratch` (`gofresh/internal/runtimeinput/scratch.go:393-395`) Fails to Elide `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` and Leaked `/tmp/gofresh-inmem-pkg-*` / `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-*.txt`**:
     - `ephemeralScratch` (`scratch.go:393-395`) elides paths under `/tmp` (`ephemeralRoots`) only when `os.Lstat(p)` returns `os.IsNotExist(err)` at observation ingest time (`Unit.Close`).
     - Because `stipulator` calls `Unit.Close` (`finishNativeRun`, `nativeexecution.go:63-90`) **before** `retainedArtifact.Close()` (`execute.go:314`) removes `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test`, `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` still exists on disk during `Unit.Close` (`os.IsNotExist(err) == false`).
     - Therefore `ephemeralScratch` returns `false`, `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` is recorded in `Fingerprint.RuntimeInputs`, `Unit.Close`'s self-check passes (because the file still exists), and 1 millisecond later `retainedArtifact.Close()` deletes `/tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test` — guaranteeing that Pass 2 fails `decideKnownGuards` with `rt.BracketMoved == true` (`runtimeinputs (moved: path /tmp/stipulator-retained-<id>/package.test)`)!
     - The exact same mechanism causes leaked `/tmp/gofresh-inmem-pkg-*` (`6,693` in `stipulator/internal/witnesscache`), leaked `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-*.txt`, and `/tmp/stipulator-telemetry-off-1000` to survive at `Unit.Close` time (`os.IsNotExist == false`) and get baked into `RuntimeInputs`.
  3. **Unisolated `~/.cache/gofresh`, `~/.cache/stipulator`, and Volatile OS Reads (`/proc`, `/sys/fs/cgroup`) in `stipulator/internal/backends/golang` Tests**:
     - Inspecting `/tmp/stipulator-testlog-447714370.txt` during `stipulator/internal/backends/golang` execution reveals:
       - `6,771 stat + 498 open` on `/home/nikolas/.cache/gofresh` and `2,994 stat + 398 open` on `/home/nikolas/.cache/stipulator` (from tests invoking `gofresh` / `stipulator` without isolating `XDG_CACHE_HOME`).
       - `1,170 open` on `/sys/fs/cgroup/user.slice` and `195 open` on `/proc/<pid>/status` and `/proc/<pid>/task/<tid>` (from tests calling `resident.Readings()`).
     - Any read of `/proc/...` or `/sys/fs/cgroup/...` hits `volatileOSPath(p)` (`testlog.go:911-936`), marking the batch observation `Unverifiable: true` (`volatile OS input: /proc/...`).
     - Any concurrent modification or cache pruning inside `/home/nikolas/.cache/gofresh` or `/home/nikolas/.cache/stipulator` during the 25-minute test run sets `observation.BracketMoved = true`, causing `Unit.Close` (`unit.go:515-517`) to return `RuntimeInputSpanError` (which even `assume_pure: true` cannot discharge) and rendering all `~464` witnesses in `internal/backends/golang` uncacheable!

#### Issue N (`stipulator/internal/backends/golang/selection.go:233-302, 358-400`, `freshness_test.go:900-1010`, `allocation_integration_test.go:168`, `admission_test.go:1126-1420`, `execute_unix_test.go:26`): `GOMAXPROCS=2` Breaks 6 Tests in `stipulator/internal/backends/golang` and `deniedTests` Triggers `553` Sequential Solo Re-Executions After the `48m12s` Batch Run
- **Evidence from `/tmp/stipulator-full-check-p1.jsonl` (`16:19:07Z`)**:
  - In `stipulator` Pass 1:
    - `35/36` packages in `race` completed in **`5m45s`** (at `12m28.6s` total wall time) and persisted **`498` witness records**.
    - The 36th package (`github.com/greatliontech/stipulator/internal/backends/golang`, `559` batch tests) ran for **`48m12s` (`binary_ms: 2,892,154`)**, generating a **`2.1 GiB` (`/tmp/stipulator-testlog-447714370.txt`)** testlog, and finished with **`disposition: "HEALTH_DISPOSITION_TEST_FAILED"`**.
  - **Why 6 of the 559 Batch Tests Failed or Skipped Under `stipulator check`**:
    1. **`TestServedRecordRevalidatesAfterTheRunsExecutions` (`freshness_test.go:900-1010`) — Deadlocked for 60s and Failed Due to `GOMAXPROCS=2` (`witnessSpawnBound(2, 2) = 1`)**:
       - `TestServedRecordRevalidatesAfterTheRunsExecutions` creates a 2-package module (`example.com/sibserve/reader` and `example.com/sibserve/writer`) where `writer`'s `TestWritesOnce` polls for 60 seconds waiting for `reader` to persist (`go.ahead`), requiring `reader` and `writer` to execute concurrently (`SpawnBound >= 2`).
       - Because the parent `stipulator check` set `GOMAXPROCS=2` in `package.test`'s environment (`witnessChildWidth = 32 / 16 = 2`), `runtime.GOMAXPROCS(0)` inside `package.test` was `2`, so `RunWitnesses` inside `TestServedRecordRevalidatesAfterTheRunsExecutions` computed `witnessSpawnBound(2, 2) = min(max(1, 2/2), 2) = 1`!
       - With `SpawnBound = 1`, `reader` and `writer` could not run concurrently; `writer` blocked for 60 seconds and failed with `t.Fatal("the reader's package never persisted")`!
    2. **`TestGoScopedAllocationWarmWidthMovement` & `TestGoScopedAllocationWarmUnobservedWidthMovement` (`allocation_integration_test.go:146-170`) — Skipped Due to `GOMAXPROCS=2` (`runtime.GOMAXPROCS(0) < 4`)**:
       - Both tests check `if runtime.GOMAXPROCS(0) < 4 { t.Skip("two package slots require at least four processors") }`. Because `stipulator check` narrowed `GOMAXPROCS=2`, both witness-bound tests skipped (`TEST_OUTCOME_SKIPPED`), denying their requirement bindings a witness!
    3. **`TestSelectionNoticesAttributeUnwalkedSelections` (`selectionnotices_test.go:34-54`) — Skipped Due to `unauditedTagGOROOT` Under `godst`**:
       - Hits `t.Skipf("the running toolchain's race selection is not admitted here...")` (`TEST_OUTCOME_SKIPPED`), denying `REQ-check-policy-notices` its witness.
    4. **`TestAdmissionsOfOneProcessShareTheHostGate` (`admission_test.go:1126-1420`) & `TestGoExecuteEnvelopeTimeoutNamesAbortedTests` (`execute_unix_test.go:26-62`) — Failed Under `GOMAXPROCS=2` + `-race`**:
       - `TestAdmissionsOfOneProcessShareTheHostGate` uses `time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)` / `time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)` after spawning goroutines via `admitAsync(...)` (`lines 1190, 1404, 1413`), assuming the goroutines have already called `readingFor(true)` and parked in `g.cond.Wait()` before `before := reads.Load()` or `cancel2()`.
       - `TestGoExecuteEnvelopeTimeoutNamesAbortedTests` sets `n.Timeout = 2*time.Second` (`line 37`) for the entire `ExecuteInvocation` (which includes `prepareNativeAttempt` -> `CaptureProducerFrame` + `engine.Snapshot` + `PrepareTestUnit` + `-race` binary startup before `=== RUN TestHang` is emitted).
  - **Catastrophic `553`-Solo-Rerun Amplification in `deniedTests` (`selection.go:233-302, 358-400`)**:
    - When `unit.run.disposition` is `HEALTH_DISPOSITION_TEST_FAILED` (because 3 tests out of `559` failed), `deniedTests` (`selection.go:381-382`) marks **every single completed passing test (`case TestOutcome_TEST_OUTCOME_PASSED: denied[name] = true`)** — all **`553` passing tests** — as "denied" and re-executes all `553` passing tests sequentially one by one in `553` separate `package.test` processes (`mode: "denied"`), taking another `~50` minutes!
    - Worse, `inSlot` (`lines 247-282`) keeps the `2.1 GiB` batch `testlog.txt` and all `553` solo `testlog.txt` files open/unclosed until `afterSlot` (`lines 287-302`), which then acquires `mu.Lock()` and calls `finishNativeRun` (`Unit.Close` -> `fromTestLog` + `checkMap` + `Validate`) `554` times sequentially — including on the already-failed `2.1 GiB` batch run!

### 10. First-Principles Bottom-Up Rearchitecture: `internal/dynstate/flowir` Before `OnceMemoType` & Cross-Package `EnvSummary` (`2026-11-13`)

- **Root-Cause Diagnosis of Pre-Refactor Chunk 2 Review Churn**:
  - Attempting to layer `onceFill` LHS consumption, `OnceMemoType`, and cross-package `EnvSummary` edges directly onto 6 parallel raw-`go/ast` intra-procedural walkers (`boundValueJudged`, `receiverReadOnlyMethods`, `enclosingConfinesRoots`, `returnEnvFreeFunctions`, `recordDynamicGlobalUses`, and `discharges_memo.go`) expanded the working tree to 53 files (`+17,022` lines) and required patching each Go surface construct (Go 1.26+ `new(expr)` vs `new(T)`, `TypeSwitchStmt` implicit case vars, addressed base chains `&((*ptr).scalar)` / `(*arrPtr)[:]`, map-key signature carriers, auto-addressed `MethodVal`s, elided-`&` composite literals, by-value `sync.Once`/`sync.Cond`, co-argument disjointness, and nested closure lexical scopes) in 3–6 places independently.
  - Note also why `dynstate` cannot rely on `go/ssa` alone: `FactOf(audited, p)` computes modular per-package summaries before importers or type instantiations exist, including uninstantiated `*types.TypeParam` generic functions whose bodies `go/ssa` leaves empty (`Blocks == nil`).
- **User-Aligned Clean-Tree Bottom-Up Sequence (Zero Sunk Cost)**:

### 14. Post-Chunk-4 `internal/dynstate` Consolidation ("Despachettification") & Domain De-Specialization Audit Results (`2026-11-15`)

Completed two parallel read-only audits across all 47 non-test files (`52,200` LOC) of `internal/dynstate` and its subpackages (`5151f238-5ab3-42a3-8c03-32ec3e9f8dd4` for `flowir` structural consolidation; `7580e253-6562-48f9-888f-3fd67b9b637d` for domain de-specialization):

#### A. Structural Consolidation (`flowir` vs Remaining Raw-`go/ast` Walkers)
1. **Baseline**: `internal/dynstate` (`22,773` LOC), `flowir` (`2,185` LOC), `refconfine` (`4,220` LOC), `freshstruct` (`5,047` LOC), `seams` (`6,632` LOC), `protoaudit` (`8,568` LOC), `syntaxutil` (`2,775` LOC).
2. **Highest-Leverage Targets (Tier 1–2, ~`2,950–3,100` LOC net reduction + 4 concrete soundness/precision fixes)**:
   - **`flowir` Kernel Completion (~25 LOC) + Per-Package `flowir` Body Cache**:
     - Support `*ast.GenDecl` in `flowir.LowerNode` (~12 LOC), record `Place.RootPos token.Pos` (~6 LOC), add `(*ValCall).CalleeSelector() *ast.SelectorExpr` (~7 LOC) for `seams` `exemptSeamNodes` and `ExplainDeferralMark` position fidelity, and cache lowered `*flowir.Body` + `ScopedMemAliases` per declaration once per `FactOfWithHooks` call (eliminating 4–6x repeated `LowerFunc`/`LowerNode` passes per `FuncDecl`).
   - **Migrate `summary_uses.go` (`2,004` LOC, 0% `flowir`) + `summary_uses_sync_opaque.go` (`629` LOC) onto `flowir` (~`750–900` LOC net reduction)**:

1. **Root Cause of `godst` Whack-a-Mole (`dst.15` vs `dst.16`/`dst.17` plain & `-tags=dst` vs vanilla Go)**:
   - `audit_testing_writer.go` (`1,863` LOC) was bespoke to `dst.15`'s `testing/dst_hostio.go` (`dstWrapTestlogWriter`), which became 100% dead weight in `dst.16`/`dst.17` when testlog writing moved to `testing/internal/testdeps/{deps.go,dst_testlog.go,dst_testlog_off.go}` + `runtime/dst_testlog_linux.go`.
   - Naive `if false` pruning alone is insufficient even for `!dst` (`StopTestLog` leaves `var dstErr error; ... if err != nil { return err }; return dstErr`), while `-tags=dst` activates `dstTestlogEnabled == true` (`var dstTestlogInBubble = dstInSimBubble` inactive runtime function-var alias + `runtime.dstWriteTestlogLine` fd-write retry loop + sticky `atomic.Uint32` errno latch).
   - **Target 4-Layer `toolchain` Semantic Kernel (Zero `go/types` Cold Penalty, Zero Go-Version Checks, Zero `dst*` Names)**:
     - **Layer 1 (`kernel_scope.go` + `kernel_inactive.go`)**: Package-generic lexical scope resolver (replacing 5 duplicate scope/shadowing walkers across `scope.go`, `offsurface.go`, `audit_os_stat.go`, `audit_os_inactive_discover.go`) + package-generic constant/inactive-gate pruner (generalizing `2,292` LOC of `os`-only inactive discovery to `testdeps`, `testlog`, `testing`, `bufio`, `os`, `runtime`, `math`, including unmutated `var fn = inactiveGate` function-variable aliases).
     - **Layer 2 (`kernel_cfg.go`)**: Intra-procedural pruned CFG + local value/error-flow abstract evaluator (eliminating hardcoded `stmts[0..k]` and `len(Body.List) == k` templates across `audit_testdeps.go`, `audit_testlog.go`, `audit_bufio.go`, `audit_testing_*.go`, and `audit_os_*.go`).
     - **Layer 3 (`sink_verify.go`)**: Unified byte-sink + `EINTR`/`EAGAIN` retry-loop + monotonic sticky-error-latch verifier replacing `audit_testing_writer.go` (`1,863` LOC deleted) and backing vanilla `*bufio.Writer`, `dst.15` custom `testing` writers, and `dst.16+` `runtime`-linknamed fd-write helpers uniformly.
     - **Layer 4 (Effect-Sequence Verifiers)**: Verify `(*os.File).Chdir` (`file_posix.go` / `file_plan9.go`), `(*testing.T).Chdir` / `(*testing.common).Chdir` (`checkParallel()`, pre-`Chdir` `oldwd`, `os.Chdir`, `PWD` `Setenv`/restore, LIFO `Cleanup` calling `oldwd.Chdir()` + `panic` on error), `runtime/debug.ReadBuildInfo`, and `math` (cleanly distinguishing **Class M1** 31 constants + 32 IEEE-754 CPU-invariant exact functions from **Class M2** `useFMA`/`hasVX` CPU-feature-dispatched transcendentals).
   - **Cohesive File Layout**: Also deduplicates the 3x `pkg/include` header loaders and 3x Go/non-Go file readers in `closure/toolchain_*.go` (`readListedPackageFiles`) and splits remaining `1,500+` LOC files cleanly below `900` LOC.
2. **Single Source of Truth in `internal/auditset`**:
   - Derive `AllowedPure{Fmt,Math,MathRand,Time,Sync,SyncAtomic,Reflect}Selectors` in `toolchain/contracts_pure.go` directly from `internal/auditset` + explicit stdlib-delegate-only deltas; move `ValuePlaneClone` (`auditedValuePlane`), deterministic compute-cone stdlib call traits (`isPureDeterministicConeStdlibCall`, `isPureMemoCall`, `harnessPureStdImports`), `%p`/`%#v` format-verb checking, and in-memory `io.Writer`/`io.Reader` surface metadata into `internal/auditset` (hashed into `auditset.Identity()`).
3. **`asmverify` (`cfg.go` + `(SB)` + `PackageSafe` / Per-`TEXT` `Verified`)**:
   - Add exact `"BT"`, `"BTW"`, `"BTL"`, `"BTQ"` to `isAsmReadOnlyCompareMnemonic`.
   - Extract preprocessor-aware `buildAsmBlockCFG` (rejecting non-returning fall-off at `TEXT` end) + GPR taint fixpoint covering both `R14` entry checks and `LEA* sym(SB)` / `MOV* $sym(SB)` lookup-table store-freedom (rejecting RMW, stores, partial-width writes, vector/stack spills).
   - Emit `ExternalSBRef{PkgPath, Name}` for non-`RODATA`/cross-package read-only `(SB)` refs and discharge in `closure/asmverify.go` via dedicated SSA carrier-free Go lookup-table immutability (`isImmutableCarrierFreeLookupGlobal`) and `init()`-populated CPU-feature global immutability (`NeedsCPUFeatures = true`).
   - Split `VerifiedPackage` into `PackageSafe` (file-level directives, `RODATA`-only `GLOBL`/`DATA`, no `init` `TEXT`), transitive per-`TEXT` `Verified` (supporting intra-package `CALL ·helper(SB)`), and `AllTextsVerified` (preserving `PackageSafe && AllTextsVerified` on whole-package write-hazard gates while gating per-subject/startup bodyless functions on `PackageSafe` + reached `Verified`).

#### B. Subsystem 2 — `dynstate`, Subpackages & `propharness` (`744d7a19-93a4-4079-9d37-245d01f57f29`, `63.3k` LOC -> `~52.5k` LOC, `-10.8k` LOC)
1. **Whole-System `unsafe` / String-Header Boundary + Deletion of 23-Field `String*` Lattice (`~-3,450` LOC)**:
   - **Real-world constraints caught up front**:

Solved mechanically with zero package allowlists via `IsomorphicValueLayout(Src, Dst)`.

#### E. First-Principles Contract Resolutions & User Sign-Off (`2026-11-16`)
1. **Q1 — Whole-System `unsafe` / String-Header Boundary**:
   - **Pure `closure` / `Observability` boundary (`0` `unsafe` fields on `dynstate.Fact`)**: `SharedDynamicStateCause` (`judgment.go:49-56`, `purity.md:100-129`, `closure.md:372-388`) requires a concrete dynamic-carrying variable `Cause.Culprit` (`<pkg>.<Var>`), whereas un-confined `unsafe` is a raw address-space write capability identical in kind to `cgo`, `.s`, and `//go:linkname` (capable of corrupting carrier-free `var Banner string` even when `len(Fact.Declares) == 0`).
   - `Check` (`MaximalClosure`) already refuses non-exempt `unsafe` binary-wide; in `CheckObserved` (`observability.go:1177`), gate the `unsafe.Pointer` sibling carve-out on `SingleSubjectExecution || !PackageHasUnconfinedUnsafeMemoryHazard(pkg)` (exempting `Sizeof`/`Alignof`/`Offsetof`, `refconfine`-verified read-only `StringData`/`Slice(StringData)`, pure scalar bitcasts, and `IsomorphicValueLayout` casts).
2. **Q2 — Compile-Time Layout Intrinsics (`unsafe.Sizeof`, `Alignof`, `Offsetof`)**:
   - By the Go spec ("Package `unsafe`"), all three are compile-time `uintptr` constants whose operands are never evaluated at runtime and whose values are 100% determined by `(MaximalClosure, Toolchain, BuildConfig)` — all unconditional code guards on both `CodeResult` and `Measurement`. Exempt in `closure` (`maximal.go` and `observability.go`) while continuing to hash the file and walk operand syntax for type/symbol dependencies.
3. **Q3 — `math` Functions & `REQ-guard-runtime-relevance` (`guards.md:158-167`, `closure.md:1741-1751`)**:
   - `CodeResult` intentionally omits `Machine` (`guard.go:74-76`) and has **no** host-CPU feature guard; admitting runtime `internal/cpu`-dispatched floating-point approximations (`math.Exp` on `amd64` via `useFMA` in `exp_amd64.s:58`, which differs by 1 ULP across CPUs under the same `GOAMD64=v1`) would violate `REQ-guard-runtime-relevance` and cause cross-CPU false-valids!
   - **Exact two-tier first-principles rule**:
     - **Tier 1 (`MaximalClosure` / `auditset` static floor)**: Admit the 31 constants + 32 IEEE-754 bit-exact `math` functions that have **zero `internal/cpu` reads on every `(GOOS, GOARCH)`**.
     - **Tier 2 (`Observability`, where `BuildConfig` code-guards `GOOS`/`GOARCH`/`GOAMD64`)**: Mechanically verify `math`'s selected Go + `.s` files in `toolchain` and admit **only** functions whose transitive Go + `.s` call graph in the selected `(GOOS, GOARCH)` has **zero runtime `internal/cpu` reads** — keeping runtime CPU-branching approximations (`Exp` and its callers on `amd64` when `useFMA` is dynamic; `hasVX` stubs on `s390x`) **refused (`Unverifiable`)**.
4. **Approved Bottom-Up Replan (`Chunks 6–14`, Committed `6520d73`, `ZERO FINDINGS` in Round 3 `99db376d`)**:
   - User confirmed replanning `docs/plans/plan.md` and `docs/issues/` around the unified whole-codebase bottom-up sequence (`Chunk 6`: `unsafe` boundary + delete 23 `String*` fields + prune 16 `syntaxutil` helpers; `Chunk 7`: `auditset` SSOT + 4-layer `toolchain` kernel + `math`/`Chdir`/`ReadBuildInfo` + `toolchain` splits; `Chunk 8`: `asmverify` `BT*` + `buildAsmBlockCFG` + `(SB)` lookup/CPU globals + `PackageSafe`/per-`TEXT` `Verified`; `Chunk 9`: `refconfine` `flowir` migration + unified `dynstate` intra-proc confinement + `AnalyzeCone` core; `Chunk 10`: `ReadOnlyVarAliases` + `StaticCompositeGlobals` + unified `flowir` memoizers + `discharges_mapping` lock regions; `Chunk 11`: name-free `LazyOnceCapsuleType`/`InitBuilderFact`/`InitSettledClusterFact` + `propharness` consolidation; `Chunk 12`: `closure` `module_inputs`/`viewload` + `directives_and_types`/`program_globals`/`ssa_confinement` + `sinkOnly` deletion -> `stream_sinks`/`MakeClosure`/`go/parser`/`-buildvcs` + cohesive `closure` splits; `Chunk 13`: streaming `testlog` + path spine + premise-gated LIFO `t.Chdir` + `testenv` cleanup; `Chunk 14`: single-pass manifest digests/movers + `CheckingUnit.close` `terminalStale` + root `gofresh` deduplications).
   - **Plan Review Refinement (`99db376d`)**: Kept `closure.Observability` and persisted `map[string]Observability` (`Proofs()`) memos execution-agnostic by computing `Observability.RequiresSingleSubject` on `tier2Base` and binding it into `ObservationProof.Evidence` (preserving `ObservationProof` JSON wire keys under `REQ-fresh-fingerprint-record`, enforcing `SingleSubjectExecution` in `admittedObservation` / `decideAfterClosureObserved`, and recording in `AcceptedAuthority`); kept `ReadBuildInfo` un-admitted until Chunk 12 wires `EffectiveBuildVCS` + `go.mod` metadata; pinned in-excursion `getenv PWD` `!pwdTruthful` sealing in Chunk 13; and scheduled `effectScanStrategy` bumps in Chunks 6 and 12.

For `math.Float64bits(math.FMA(0, math.Inf(1), math.NaN()))` on `amd64` `GOAMD64=v1`, `HasFMA=true` (`VFMADD213SD`) preserves `+qNaN` (`0x7ff8000000000001`) while `HasFMA=false` (`GODEBUG=cpu.fma=off` or non-FMA CPU) executes `0*Inf + z` at `fma.go:100`, flipping sign bit 63 to `-qNaN` (`0xfff8000000000000`) without changing `(Toolchain, BuildConfig)`. Therefore `math.FMA` is **excluded** at both Tier 1 and Tier 2 (`floor_amd64.s` / `roundToEven` vs `ROUNDSD` match bit-for-bit on all finite numbers, `±0.0`, `±Inf`, and quiet `math.NaN()`).

### 19. `gofresh` Chunk 8 Pre-Implementation Soundness Design (`2026-11-17`)

1. **Explicit & Entailed Invariants Derived Before Coding**:
   - **I1 (`BT*` Exact Match)**: Add exact `"BT"`, `"BTW"`, `"BTL"`, `"BTQ"` to `isAsmReadOnlyCompareMnemonic` (`isa.go`) — never `HasPrefix("BT")` (which would collide with RMW `BTS*`/`BTR*`/`BTC*`).
   - **I2 (`buildAsmBlockCFG` Non-Returning Fall-Off Rejection)**: Every `TEXT` block builds a preprocessor-aware (`#if`/`#elif`/`#else`/`#endif`) and macro-choice (`line.choices`) statement CFG (`cfg.go`) with a dedicated `falloffExit` node (also wired from positive `+k(PC)` relative jumps unless `k <= 0` or proven not to overshoot `RET`); any path from `entry` reaching `falloffExit` rejects the `TEXT`, preventing fallthrough execution into an adjacent unreached/impure `TEXT` in `.text`.
   - **I3 (Default-Deny `(SB)`-Address GPR + Flags Taint Fixpoint)**: Over `asmBlockCFG`, track a 32-bit monotone taint mask (`GPRs` + bit 31 `flagsTainted`) for any register receiving `$sym(SB)` or `LEA* ... sym(SB)`. Allow only (a) pure memory loads `off(Rt)...` in read-only operand positions (reading scalar table data without `.P`/`.W` writeback), (b) GPR-to-GPR copies and `LEA*`/`ADD*` pointer-offset arithmetic into GPRs, and (c) full-width untainted overwrites / `XOR R, R` self-zeroing. Reject all memory/stack/`(FP)` stores of or through tainted GPRs (closing both direct `.s` table clobbers and returning `[]byte`/`*T` aliases to Go callers), vector register smuggles, conditional branches/`CMOV*`/`SET*`/`CSEL*`/`CBZ`/`TBZ` on tainted flags or GPRs (closing ASLR address-bit entropy leaks), and tainted GPRs live into inter-`TEXT` `CALL`/`JMP` while propagating callee `retTaintMask` back to `CALL`/`BL` sites.
   - **I4 (`PackageSafe` / Per-`TEXT` `Verified` / `AllTextsVerified` Split & Duplicate/Static `TEXT` Isolation)**:
     - File-static `helper<>(SB)` `TEXT`s are keyed per file (`file#i:<>helper`) so sibling `.s` files cannot cross-call or shadow each other's static helpers; duplicate `TEXT ·Foo(SB)` blocks across `#ifdef` arms conservatively join (`Verified = prev && cur`, unioning `ExternalSBRefs` and `ImpureKind`).
     - `PackageSafe && AllTextsVerified` is preserved on all whole-package write-hazard gates (`pkgFreeOfUnverifiedNativeWriteHazard`, `nonSSAGlobalHazards` `blockedPkgs`, and `verifiedPackageAssemblyMaximalPure`), while `addReachedPackageFiles`, `tier2PackageExternalEffects` (`subjectFiltered=true`), `verifiedPackageAssemblyFunc`, and `isVerifiedPureRootedAsmStub` (`safeAsm`) gate on `PackageSafe` + per-`TEXT` `Verified` + discharged `ExternalSBRefs`.
   - **I5 (`ExternalSBRef` Two-Channel Discharge & `REQ-closure-coverage` Object Enqueue)**:
     - Reject `internal/cpu.X86` / `internal/cpu.ARM64` (mutated by unlogged `GODEBUG` in `runtime.schedinit` before `testlog` starts), admitting `internal/cpu.HWCap` when zero non-std `//go:linkname` writers exist.
     - For non-std `ExternalSBRef{PkgPath, Name}`, verify across **all** loaded compartments of `PkgPath` (`b.idxByTypes` / `p.Pkgs`, including `_test.go` variants and uninstantiated generics) that `v` is a pointer-free, slice-free, carrier-free value global (`Basic`/`Array`/`Struct`), free of `//go:linkname`, `flagBacked`, and un-confined package `unsafe`, with zero writes outside `PkgPath`'s synchronous `init` cone and zero `&`/`[:]` escapes anywhere in SSA or AST. Enqueue `v` in `tier2Analyzer` (`enqueueObject(v)`) so cross-package lookup-table files participate in Tier-2 closure digests.

   - `F3 (HIGH)` (`internal/closure/internal/asmverify/asmverify.go`): Restricted `Verified: true` on `ImpureKind != ""` instructions strictly to register-only read-only counter/RNG queries (`isRegisterOnlyImpureReadInstruction`: `RDTSC`, `RDTSCP`, `RDPMC`, `XTEST`, GPR-dest `RDPID`/`RDRAND*`/`RDSEED*`, non-canonical `CPUID`, and `arm64` `MRS`/`MOVD <sysreg>, Rd` into a clean GPR); unverified raw opcodes (`BYTE`/`WORD`/`DWORD`/`LONG`/`QUAD` outside `0f 01 d0` `XGETBV` and `isAuditedARM64WordOpcode`) and system/syscall/state-mutating instructions (`SYSCALL`, `SVC`, `MSR`, `MovdWriteFpcr`, etc.) set `Verified: false` (`AllTextsVerified: false`) while preserving `ImpureKind` on `vp.Funcs[ent.goName]`.
